## `AFG-4100-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Concrete NDT Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Mass Concrete Ultrasonic Void & Rebar Coupler Mechanical Splicing
- **Intro:** Concrete NDT Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Mass Concrete Ultrasonic Void & Rebar Coupler Mechanical Splicing — Pre-Calibration Equipment Check and Surface Preparation: Battery voltage ≥ 11.5 V DC; transducer frequency 54 kHz ± 1 kHz; surface roughness ≤ 0.5 mm; test point spacing ± 2 mm.
- **Key tolerance:** Battery voltage ≥ 11.5 V DC; transducer frequency 54 kHz ± 1 kHz; surface roughness ≤ 0.5 mm; test point spacing ± 2 mm.
- **Standards:** `ISO 1920-7:2004`, `ASTM C597-2016 - Standard Test Method for Pulse Velocity Through`, `ACI 228.2`, `ISO 15803:2002`

## `AGO-4100-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Concrete Pump Lead
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Super High-Rise Self-Climbing Hydraulic Formwork & Concrete Pump
- **Intro:** Concrete Pump Lead — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Super High-Rise Self-Climbing Hydraulic Formwork & Concrete Pump — Verify Permit-to-Work and LOTO Status: Permit validity: current date and shift time. LOTO tags must be present on all isolation points. No missing locks or tags.
